# Waveshare ESP32-S3 boards

The 94 profiles below support 2.4 GHz Wi-Fi and Bluetooth LE. Each board links its EspHub application, hardware documentation and manufacturer examples. Flash capacity is in MiB.

## Manufacturer examples

Each link below points to the original source. Archive-based examples include the path inside the download. Hardware-specific peripherals, revision labels and upstream toolchains are those of the manufacturer. EspHub-buildable examples appear in each board’s linked profile.
